## Appendix B. Live counterfactual messages: conditional content and route credit

The prior replay learns route utility from detached alternative losses, while the realized winner supplies content gradients. This need not be biased, but losing message functions receive no direct payload derivative on that realization. The new training-only sibling differentiates through every alternative of ONE sampled race, including complete live prefixes and private writes.

At a uniformly sampled legal race r, objective = sum stopgrad(pi_i)*L_i + R*sum pi_i*stopgrad(L_i), averaged over actual examples. The branch-content average REPLACES factual loss gradient; adding both would double-count. Only sampled choice credit gets legal-race scaling R. Native computational delays, factorized first-time gradients, sparse private state, key/value separation and inference remain unchanged.

For ONE episode at fixed entering history/first time and independent future draws, the branch average is the conditional mean of the native pathwise estimator. It removes current-winner variance at that site. Conditioning on the entire candidate noise would reveal the winner. Shared noise across episodes means this is not a lower BATCH-variance theorem. Global clipping and Adam are nonlinear; their expected updates need not be preserved.

One shared native forward response supplies feature geometry and supervised feedback for many candidate routes. The credit model transports their alternative lexical parameters and Adam moments instead of fitting every alternative independently. Full factual correction preserves the issued route-gradient expectation.

### A connected controller receives a better signal

In a fixed 37,377-parameter route controller, transport reduces conditional action-gradient noise by 77.8% versus static credit. Expected parameter gradients match the exact route objective within 1.08e-19. Four earlier prefixes; this measures action noise, not variation across future sequences or optimization speed.

The working prototype already carries selected keys, lexical parameters and optimizer state between route-learning windows. Early 512-target controller fits have no confirmed held-out task gain. The next decisive test is better held-out prediction per total learning cost.

| --- | --- | --- | --- |

Transport plus exact factual correction runs 2.09x faster with 93.5% fewer supported operations than exhaustive independent alternative learning. Both include the same native context, candidate discovery and normalized outputs. Transport predicts the alternatives approximately; observed factual/reference losses are exact.

Four earlier prefixes, 48 targets each, three rotated timing repeats. Median execution per prefix plus common acquisition; 192 scored targets per representative pipeline. All keys/vocabulary work, optimizer state, selected actual learner, copies and correction paid. Unsupported scalar/special/optimizer arithmetic omitted from supported FLOPs and included in wall. Repeated measurements, exact-oracle acquisition, source pretraining and contracts/smokes additional. Acquisition economy, not matched-quality task-learning speed or hardware energy.

### Future learning changes the right route

In the native 48-target probe, direct addressed-output effects vanish while learner-state effects persist. Three of four prefixes have later cumulative effects opposing the first eight targets; one offsets 30.1% of the initial loss increase. The best route changes between 8- and 48-target objectives in three of four all-route calibration prefixes.

Credit must carry learning influence as well as physical message decay. Optimizer transport and a connected downstream critic provide complementary ways to represent that influence.

### Conditional platform potential

- Economical continual adaptation as language, processes and environments change.
- Local industrial and embodied systems with constrained learning-state budgets.
- Reusable credit across routes and modalities, with a sparse asynchronous hardware path.

These are conditional application and economic implications. Customer adoption requires useful quality, reliability and measured complete deployment cost. Hardware energy, traffic and depth scaling require their own measurements.

### Compression and depth: the next formal target

Compress task-relevant influence on future learning/loss, with weights, moments, activations and precision charged. Low-dimensional state does not imply a few-bit or optimal representation. For the restricted head, independent alternatives cost O(WVd), shared reference plus transport O(Vd+Wd). Full-carrier learning has additional cross-layer work.

Depth errors obey a local amplification-plus-residual recurrence. Connected credit geometry, joint downstream-loss training, long-range anchors and adaptive exact corrections can reduce amplification and local error. Legitimate signal gains must be retained; stability cannot be obtained by discarding useful credit.

### Promotion gates

- Better held-out prediction per total learning work on the token protocol.
- Quality/work curves across data, depth, width and candidate capacity.
- Reproducible online application evaluation, then measured target-hardware memory, latency, traffic and energy.

Evidence and formulas: investment/forward_credit_evidence_20261011.json. Theory: OPTIMIZER_STATE_TRANSPORT_CREDIT, STATE_INFLUENCE_BOUNDARY_CRITIC and LEARNED_DEPTH_CREDIT_REPRESENTATIONS. No pending cell is presented as a result.

### Depth learning: forward probes and joint error structure

Forward responses supply backward-projection teaching targets by reciprocity. Isotropic probe risk equals cotangent squared error; residual projection correction has the exact mean, but one Rademacher probe in n dimensions has variance(n-1) times residual squared norm. Connected predictors and amplification-aware sparse audits must reduce this burden. Joint depth learning should also reduce transported coherent bias and harmful cross-layer error covariance; local MSE alone does not optimize those terms.

For a common issued binary-outcome belief independent of the route draw, score-gradient error has L1 bound |belief error| times logit range/2, with no candidate-count or neural-depth factor. Constructive transfer/stability/future-learning theory: experiments/theory/RECIPROCAL_CREDIT_TRANSFER_STABILITY_FUTURE_LEARNING.md and report/reciprocal_credit_theory.pdf. Current-state packet geometry transports reusable influence; audits contract credit-tracking error without erasing forward memory; future-demand-weighted information gain selects useful exploration. For a quadratic future loss with predictable update geometry, weighted correction-variance reduction gives an exact expected future-loss improvement. These sufficient conditions define the next native integration.

Whole-native future-taught learning: actor and controller learn from the same actual forward/backward passes. Pointer selected by aggregate5-split validation,TEST once. Sources/model checkpoints match;causality and mark-normalization contracts pass. Github memory repair passes at1072656KiB without history truncation;only6failed fits are restored on slots5/6,with exact pass recovery and automatic validation-selection/TEST publication. Stack Overflow finishes its admitted split4 fits,then frozen once-only scoring. [Current evidence](../tpp/B4_DEVELOPED_CURRENT.md);[remaining programme](../BENCHMARK_CONTINUATION.md).

The log-domain clock repair completes 75 TRAIN passes with zero skipped updates, versus 60 skips in the saved guarded run. Selected DEV total loss improves by 2.607 nats per sequence. The original guarded run skipped a padded batch containing most TRAIN events.

| Model | Time loss | Mark loss | Total loss |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| Repaired baseline | -181.381 | 67.237 | -114.144 |
| Plus mark memory | -181.754 | 65.417 | -116.337 |

The mark-memory variant improves selected mark loss by 1.820 and total loss by 2.193. Its registered stability gate fails: the last 20 common epochs improve marks by 0.687, below the required 1.000; total gain is 1.061. No adoption or family-loss verdict follows.

At the selected mark checkpoint, disabling only its mark read raises mark loss from 65.417 to 79.239. The direct read helps 28 of 34 sequences and gains 13.822 nats per sequence, with timing unchanged. The residual cross-fit difference includes shared learning and RNG changes; it is not a separately identified causal effect.

| Arm | Passes | Scored TRAIN targets | Fit minutes | ms / target |
| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |
| mark_memory | 64 | 851,648 | 61.58 | 4.339 |
| repaired_baseline | 75 | 998,025 | 55.76 | 3.352 |

Parameters: 21,768 baseline, 29,057 mark variant. Both score every clock; the auxiliary temporal memory updates densely. Whole fitting FLOPs are unmeasured. Saved DEV likelihood-forward latency is 73.03 versus 86.41 microseconds per target; no matched-compute or energy claim.

Scope: one Github split1 seed0 TRAIN/DEV development comparison, original core recipe and validation patience. Frozen B4 TEST verdicts remain unchanged.

Frozen-core residual fitting completes 43 passes, with zero skips and unchanged timing, but fails its development gate: selected mark gain 0.169; last-20 mean -0.536. Residual fitting 646.58s; cumulative fitting including the repaired warm start 3992.29s.

Checkpoint swaps show that the joint branch transfers poorly to the repaired core. Preserving its original mark-input embedding/gap weights changes transferred DEV mark loss only 71.615 to 71.592, versus repaired baseline 67.237. The input-representation hypothesis is not supported by this diagnostic. Late residual training improves TRAIN loss while worsening DEV loss.

A TRAIN-only scalar confidence fit combines the two complete native conditional-mark predictors with coefficient 0.613305, retaining the repaired timing density. DEV mark loss 65.203 versus baseline 67.237, gain 2.034; total loss -116.178. One coefficient is used for every candidate mark; there is no target-dependent inference selection.

Scalar fitting 18.53ms after 2.941s of predictor evaluation. Both native models remain necessary: 50,826 parameters and 7040.78s of prerequisite fits are charged. This is a saved-predictor calibration diagnostic, not an integrated sparse-substrate or efficiency win. Neither failed mark-fit gate is relaxed. The completed confirmation and single-readout results follow.

Fixed-recipe three-seed calibration confirmation passes: mean DEV mark gain 1.979 nats per sequence, sample SD 0.537. Registered gate: mean gain >=1.000 and every seed positive; TRAIN-only global coefficients, repaired timing unchanged.

## Appendix. B10 online market: preregistered October confirmation

**Win (single seed), passed preregistered out-of-sample confirmation.** On 1 October 2026 Binance BTCUSDT public trades (799,179 events), acquired after the preregistration `experiments/market/B10_OCTOBER_CONFIRMATION.json` was committed, the native online learner beats the online LSTM and both online Transformer policies. Selection and settings come from August only; there is no October tuning. Each arm carries its complete August learner, numerical memory and Adam state across the calendar gap, observes 128 October context events, then issues 65,536 forecasts and updates its weights after every 32 revealed outcomes. Each forecast is scored before its outcome can change weights or state.

| Model (all adapt online) | Oct 1 NLL, 65,536 forecasts | Gain of native | Scored-stream arithmetic GF | Ratio to native |
| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |
| **Native temporal learner** | **8.078536** | — | **8.509** | 1.00x |
| LSTM | 8.102707 | 0.024171 | 9.510 | 1.12x |
| Transformer, cached past-version KV | 8.179195 | 0.100659 | 124.682 | 14.65x |
| Transformer, history re-encoded after each update | 8.176375 | 0.097839 | 7963.123 | 935.89x |

Quarter-prefix NLL at 16,384/32,768/49,152/65,536 forecasts: native 7.765531/7.973383/7.988078/8.078536; LSTM 7.794729/8.001260/8.013996/8.102707; cached Transformer 7.886069/8.083982/8.091900/8.179195; re-encoded Transformer 7.877936/8.078957/8.088360/8.176375. Native has the lowest NLL in all 16 of 16 consecutive 4,096-forecast windows. The ranking and the margin over LSTM (0.021 August, 0.024 October) hold across the five-week gap.

The August development stream extends the same learners exactly from 16,384 to 65,536 forecasts: native 5.175881, LSTM 5.196725, re-encoded Transformer 5.279655, cached Transformer 5.280432. Retained weights plus numerical prediction state: native 0.146 MB vs cached Transformer 2.334 MB (16.0x; logical tensor bytes at reference precision, optimizer state separate). Native guidance with repaired parity (source actor 3.46e−12) adds nothing on the 16,384-forecast stream (5.733357878 exact BPTT vs 5.733358893 online guidance) and is not promoted; the factual online learner is the fighter.

## Appendix. R1 native language: lane, memory-boundary and depth attribution

**In development: best native learner 5.978388 nats/token** (8,192 reused DEV targets, 1,024-token history, seed 6), from 6.065563 two stages earlier. Each stage is a single-factor 2M-target continuation (4,096 updates) of the strongest acquired learner, with eight contiguous fresh-TRAIN streams, persistent numerical state, gradient window 16, the same calibrated caps/moments/teacher, and full capacity, keys, values and writes.

| Stage decision | Arm | DEV NLL | Prequential TRAIN NLL | Training s |
| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |
| Lane clock policy (parent 6.065563) | **Shared lanes** | **6.010525** | 5.944408 | 2037.2 |
| | Independent lanes | 6.021640 | 5.942668 | — |
| Memory at document boundary (parent 6.010525) | **Reset at corpus EOS in training** | **5.978388** | 5.912153 | 2038.9 |
| | Memory persists across EOS | 5.996434 | 5.917253 | 2008.9 |

Both scored arms run without EOS reset at DEV. Shared lane clocks win DEV by 0.011 with prequential TRAIN tied. Carrying state across unrelated documents during training costs 0.018 DEV and 0.005 prequential at this budget: memory inside a document is useful, but the persistent arm has not learned to forget at the boundary. R1 continuations adopt shared lanes and the corpus-EOS training boundary; a learned EOS gate is the test for returning boundary handling to learning. Stage gains include the added training; arm differences are the attributions.

Protected depth growth: the grown D4 learner (6.095077) trails the continued D2 parent (6.065563) by 0.029514. The frozen attribution splits this into harmful learned correction 0.022399 and own-parent-path excess 0.007115. Erasing the added stage's private history changes NLL by 0.000166, so the grown branch's memory is not used. The next depth step repairs output/branch learning (TRAIN-only parent-safe convex correction, `theory/PARENT_SAFE_CONVEX_TEMPORAL_GROWTH.md`) before any further depth fit. The native deep D8 acquisition at 32M TRAIN tokens continues on AWS.

## Appendix. G-HIER deep representational composition implementation

### Native multi-memory composition learns a usable FIT signal

Eight abstraction levels, four native race stages per level, 169,675 parameters.
Persistent indexed objects integrate child events and query responses from all
active lower learned memories in their support. The final round has no raw-input
shortcut. Reads preserve child state; root publication is generated at a native
race time. Query selection, ownership and arming policies remain explicit.

On 12 distinct 32-event prefixes from one official FIT recording, 64 batch-one
updates reduce paired stochastic NLL 2.547975 → 2.482746. Two common noise tapes
are shared across all episodes, with bitwise train/evaluation forward parity.
Label association rises −0.013494 → +0.037650; it is positive on both tapes.
Raw accuracy reaches the majority predictor's 16.67%; raw NLL remains above the
empirical constant-prior 2.369382. This prompted a frozen readout diagnosis,
not another unchanged backbone fit.

| Frozen construction | Raw NLL | Prior-centred FIT NLL | FIT accuracy |
| --- | ---: | ---: | ---: |
| Initial core and head | 2.547975 | 2.369382 | 16.67% |
| Trained core and head | 2.482746 | **1.925817** | **37.50%** |
| Trained, query contents zeroed | 2.452741 | 2.369382 | 16.67% |
| Initial core, trained head | 2.541522 | 2.369382 | 16.67% |
| Trained core, initial head | 2.461518 | **1.926302** | **37.50%** |

Scope: one FIT recording, two stochastic tapes, no DEV/TEST access. Prior,
logit centre and one nonnegative scalar gain are fitted and scored on these same
FIT logits. These are optimistic learnability results, not generalization or a
semantic hierarchy win. Swaps measure these fixed decoders, not a basis-invariant
ranking. They locate a useful learned signal in the native core for this probe;
removing lower-memory content removes that gain. No per-item noise identifier is
provided. The calibrated artifact changes only the external linear readout.

The loss identity L = H(p) + mean KL(p || q) − A explains why erasing useful
query content can improve raw loss: content adds 0.051704 label association and
0.081709 label-independent penalty. Prior-centred logits expose the signal.
For q(g)=softmax(log p + g(z−mean z)), convex FIT loss has derivative −A at g=0.
This guarantees a positive-gain FIT improvement when A>0, not held-out transfer.

### Native learning and execution costs

64 training presentations contain 2,048 raw event presentations; unique input
contains 384 events. Fitting plus all scheduled scoring took 961.276 CPU seconds,
peak RSS 2,074,196 KiB. Frozen five-way diagnosis took 164.565 seconds and
297,376 KiB RSS. These are implementation diagnostics, not hardware estimates.
For the measured first episode: 79 objects, 78 lower-memory queries, 334 delivered
messages/publications, 10,688 candidate scores and 5,344 selected native updates.
Learning evaluates 10,688 counterfactual values; selected-only evaluation does
not. Two timer candidates are scored. Full fitting FLOPs, per-target arithmetic,
traffic and hardware energy remain unaudited. State tensor counters exclude
graphs, input records, queues, aliases, parameters and Python metadata.

### Formal and implementation advances

State-ownership contracts protect lower published states from a changed private
upper hypothesis. This is inference-state independence, not independence from
shared parameter learning. Native coupled value/time credit passes an integrated
expectation identity for affine selected-value losses; nonlinear value losses
retain a local surrogate. Do not modify its timing derivative in isolation.

`VarianceReducedNativeComposition` preserves native parameters, random draws,
forward outputs, publication times and event work. Only pure emission-timer
credit changes: average the winner indicator over its conditional race law,
removing winner variance from the interior timing gradient. Receipt/value races
retain their coupled teacher. This does not repair event-order discontinuities
or establish a training-quality improvement. See the linked mathematical notes.


Scope: FIT-only development. No held-out semantic hierarchy or benchmark result. The earlier synchronous stencil result and its restricted top-level access remain in [current findings](../experiments/hierarchy/CURRENT_FINDINGS.md), alongside complete source-bound evidence and the next held-out decision.

### Coalesced native composition resource gate

On the same32-event TRAIN scene and acquired weights, separating local publication from upward race-generated child views reduces delivered messages349→125, native candidate scores11,168→4,000 and selected updates5,584→2,000. Timer scores rise2→188 and emissions1→94. All eight levels receive finite nonzero content gradients; native32stage structure and parameters remain. This is changed information flow, not an equal-quality comparison. Process peak RSS across the pair1,350,556KiB, software forward/backward10.286s→4.231s; full arithmetic/traffic and hardware energy are unaudited. Eager adjacent-frontier v1 was stopped by the3GB watchdog at3,202,220KiB.

Controlled composition task audit passes:128TRAIN/64DEV/64TEST scenes, three nested arrangement levels, familiar parts but split-disjoint combinations and nuisance tapes. A single child plus root orientation is label-balanced. Whole-label128update native learning completes but fails unseen-combination DEV: raw NLL1.163353, scalar-calibrated NLL2.508095,50%accuracy versus prior0.693147/50%. Fixed TRAIN-ridge affine-head audit also fails (initial/final DEV551.710/216.153). Cached final root features react353x more to nuisance-pair changes than to root-relation/label changes; both translation and timing differ. TRAIN occupancy94objects versus DEV127/106 motivates a frozen2x2 coordinate/timing audit. It localizes translation-only shift0.416171 versus timing-only0.000228. New world-pixel persistence/observed-frame parent composition removes pure-translation feature shift exactly, reduces combined shift0.416184→0.003158, and retains all32native stages/169378parameters. Native gate150deliveries,4800candidate scores,238timer scores,119objects,104320state bytes,finite gradients at every level;76contracts pass. Repaired128update whole-label fit completes with unchanged split/order/optimizer: raw DEV1.297693,TRAIN-only scalarcalibration gain0 gives0.693147/50%accuracy. Fixed TRAIN ridge.01 readout initial/final DEV0.732992/0.743684,42.19%/46.88%; no transfer gain. TEST not model-scored; initial/final feature caches retained. Process632.316s,peak743168KiBRSS,training614400native candidates/307200selected updates/614400counterfactual evaluations/30464timer scores/19200deliveries. Next repair targets part/role binding and credit; no larger unchanged fit. TEST remains unscored by the model. Fitting plus scheduled scoring545.072s, peak RSS684,948KiB. ### Paired native role-memory composition
